# Appier Enterprise Console

Appier's enterprise console provides a seamless and consistent user experience, serving as the centralized, cross-product management platform for Appier's full-funnel marketing solutions, including AIQUA, BotBonnie and AIRIS. The console provides:

  * An organization-wide view of all your projects and products.
  * Cross-product common settings and channel integrations.

![](https://files.readme.io/9fb3d5116a3c8811f540864b8a3e107a2576f24f9201f0ec83ef632e1296ad21-enterprise-account.png)

Access your organization's account management and common settings directly from any product console by clicking the app launcher in the navigation sidebar.

![](https://files.readme.io/edade65ad91fbfc949755267f6e7749cbb04330f11439cffd40af9e0302f2fbc-enterprise-common-settings.png)

To learn more about how to use enterprise console, refer to the following guides:

  * **Account & Usage**
    * [Projects](doc:account-management-projects)
    * [My account](doc:account-management-my-account)
  * **Common settings**
    * [Channels](doc:common-settings-channels)
    * [Partner Integrations](doc:common-settings-integration-partner-integrations)
    * [E-Commerce Integrations](doc:common-settings-integration-e-commerce-integrations)
    * [SDK](doc:common-settings-integrations-sdks)



__Updated 7 months ago

* * *

What’s Next

Read on to learn more about how to use the Appier Enterprise Console.

# My Account

## 

Overview

To manage your account details, select the app launcher, then choose **Account & Usage** > **My account** from the navigation sidebar.

  * Edit your name and picture
  * Reset your password

![](https://files.readme.io/902e5659d2c50be682215e3d7026e8e73aed95e7df001c1f8c9fac9bb2d1665d-my-account-overview.png)

* * *

## 

Edit your name and picture

Click the edit icon next to your name or camera icon to edit your profile name and picture, then click **Save**.

![](https://files.readme.io/427d9316dae388eedbab37cc74a102cc9774201947de6d1ab9f5e6d2436a587f-edit-account-info.png)

## 

Reset your password

To reset your password, click **Reset Password** , and a password reset link will be sent to the email address associated with your account.

![](https://files.readme.io/28a4416c91747510c93293c88ac68294d988d77a3ca5546d08a0d304cc9cefe6-reset-account.png)

__Updated about 1 year ago

* * *

# Projects

## 

Overview

To view all the projects associated to your organization, navigate to the app launcher in the navigation sidebar, click **Account & Usage** to access to the **Projects** page.

  * The organization is the top-level entity in the Appier Enterprise Console.
  * Each organization can contain multiple projects.
  * Each project can contain multiple products (such as AIQUA, BotBonnie).

![](https://files.readme.io/1a5e13e32586972150961c1dae79362081f3e8d15ff4623e8178bd200f9284a0-project-overview.png)

* * *

## 

Organization

Organizations are the top-level entity and can contain multiple projects.

![](https://files.readme.io/257c548642ab3b0293f22adc468b0e45f991fb79f3328f10e52bf80eaf5555e4-home-organization.png)

* * *

## 

Projects

Each project has a name and ID and can contain multiple products in an organization.

![](https://files.readme.io/441e75bb1c1d3b4902a24dd99e546f5ef07a17b2b568e75ad08ebe5dfa9f6e10-home-project.png)

* * *

## 

Products

You can view each product's details, including its name, product-specific ID (for example, AIQUA ID, BotBonnie bot ID), and additional information, such as the number of seats available for that product.

![](https://files.readme.io/543d8c01caeb0405c2ce8522098e01cf19a6eda5b51324cb403750bc52e021df-home-products.png)

__Updated about 1 year ago

* * *

# Add Journey Nodes

## 

Overview

Each node in the journey map is a step in the user journey, and each node contains settings related to its specific purpose. For example, a message node contains campaign creative settings, while a split node contains logic for splitting users into different paths.

The first step in creating your journey is to add an [entry trigger node](doc:entry-trigger-node), which specifies how users enter the journey. Next, continue customizing your journey by:

  * Adding nodes
  * Editing nodes
  * Copying nodes
  * Deleting nodes
  * Undoing and redoing changes



* * *

## 

Add nodes

To add a node, click on the **+** that appears in the journey map. After clicking the add button, you'll be able to select one of the following node types:

  * **Entry trigger node** : Specify how users will enter the journey. Only available as the first node in the journey. For details, see [Entry Trigger Node](doc:entry-trigger-node).
  * **Message** : Reach out to users across different channels. In a message node, you can set up the creative you want to send. For details, see [Message Node](doc:message-node).
  * **Split** : Specify a condition that splits users into different paths. For details, see [Split Node](doc:split-node).
  * **Wait** : Wait for a certain amount of time before continuing to the next node. For details, see [Wait Node](doc:wait-node).
  * **Update users** : Update user-related information. For details, see [Update Users Node](doc:update-users-node).

![](https://files.readme.io/99e397c-jm-add-node.png)

* * *

## 

Edit nodes

To edit a node, simply click on it, and its settings panel will open on the right. Then, you can modify its settings and click **Save** to apply the changes.

![](https://files.readme.io/f30020e-jm-edit-existing-node.png)

### 

Comparison operators

Some nodes support comparison operators that evaluate user events and attributes. For example:

  * **Wait for condition** nodes allow you pause users at a specific step in the journey until they satisfy a condition, such as completing a `checkout_completed` event for a product with an ID equal to `123`.
  * **Check condition** nodes allow you to split users to different paths depending on the conditions they satisfy, for example, splitting users to different paths based on their membership level.



Different comparison operators are available for strings and numbers.

![](https://files.readme.io/92acc7b2fc1d34ffec20f824fa2441232a629cf0cbd604ad14df08b67c2de9df-comparison-operators.gif)

#### 

String comparison operators

Operator| Usage| Example  
---|---|---  
**is one of**|  True if the operand is an exact match of at least one of the specified values.Specifying multiple comma-separated values is supported.| `deviceType` is one of `mobile`, `tablet`  
**equals**|  True if the operand matches the specified value exactly.| `product_id` equals `A123`  
**exists**|  True if the operand exists, regardless of its value. Note that empty values also evaluate to true.| `custom_attribute` exists  
**contains**|  True if the operand contains the specified value.You can only specify one value to compare—specifying multiple values is not supported.| `product_category` contains `electronics`  
**matches regex**|  True if the operand matches the specified regular expression.Regular expressions are patterns used to match character combinations in strings.| `email` matches regex `^\[a-zA-Z0-9.\_%+-]+@(example.org\|example.com)$`  
  
#### 

Numerical comparison operators

Operator| Usage  
---|---  
`≥`| True if the operand is greater than or equal to the specified value.  
`≤`| True if the operand is less than or equal to the specified value.  
`>`| True if the operand is greater than the specified value.  
`<`| True if the operand is less than the specified value.  
`=`| True if the operand is equal to the specified value.  
`exists`| True if the operand exists, regardless of its value.  
`between`| True if the operand falls within the specified range (inclusive).  
  
* * *

## 

Delete nodes

To delete a node, hover over it and click the trash can.

![](https://files.readme.io/44e6169-jm-delete-node1.png)

Next, choose how to handle the path after the node you're deleting, then click **Confirm**.

The available deletion options will differ depending on the node type. For example, if you're deleting a message node, you can choose whether to delete the path for reachable users, the path for non-reachable users, or both.

![](https://files.readme.io/c2b3777-jm-delete-node2.png)

* * *

## 

Copy nodes

To copy a node and its subsequent branches, hover over the node and click on the copy button. After clicking the copy button, you can copy the node into any of the available positions shown in the journey.

![](https://files.readme.io/7d1e336-jm-copy-node.png)

* * *

## 

Undo and redo changes

In the bottom right corner of the canvas, you'll find undo and redo buttons, which allow you to revert or reapply the following operations:

  * Adding a node
  * Deleting a node
  * Copying and pasting a node

![](https://files.readme.io/4d6b07f-jm-undo-redo.png)

__Updated 9 months ago

* * *

[Journey Copilot](/docs/journey-copilot)[Entry Trigger Node](/docs/entry-trigger-node)

# Appier Enterprise Console

Appier's enterprise console provides a seamless and consistent user experience, serving as the centralized, cross-product management platform for Appier's full-funnel marketing solutions, including AIQUA, BotBonnie and AIRIS. The console provides:

  * An organization-wide view of all your projects and products.
  * Cross-product common settings and channel integrations.

![](https://files.readme.io/9fb3d5116a3c8811f540864b8a3e107a2576f24f9201f0ec83ef632e1296ad21-enterprise-account.png)

Access your organization's account management and common settings directly from any product console by clicking the app launcher in the navigation sidebar.

![](https://files.readme.io/edade65ad91fbfc949755267f6e7749cbb04330f11439cffd40af9e0302f2fbc-enterprise-common-settings.png)

To learn more about how to use enterprise console, refer to the following guides:

  * **Account & Usage**
    * [Projects](doc:account-management-projects)
    * [My account](doc:account-management-my-account)
  * **Common settings**
    * [Channels](doc:common-settings-channels)
    * [Partner Integrations](doc:common-settings-integration-partner-integrations)
    * [E-Commerce Integrations](doc:common-settings-integration-e-commerce-integrations)
    * [SDK](doc:common-settings-integrations-sdks)

* * *

# Choose a Journey Creation Method

Go to **Journey maps** and click **\+ Create journey map** to choose a journey creation method.

![](https://files.readme.io/3ceb2fe0d5eae98ba9ac88d96ee2f988d8fea48a0345988cc4942f88959e2d3f-create-journey-map.png)

After adding a new journey map from scratch or from a template, you can also your AI-powered assistant [journey copilot](doc:journey-copilot) to automatically generate a journey map based on a text description of the desired user journey.

![](https://files.readme.io/fe2aa3a-jm-copilot-prompt.png)

* * *

## 

1\. Choose a creation method

  * **Start from scratch**: Start with a blank canvas and manually add nodes to construct a user journey.
  * **Choose a template**: Start with a prebuilt template based on a specific use case, such as "New User Registration" or "First Purchase incentive, then modify the specifics to suit your needs.



### 

Start from scratch

To start with a blank canvas, click **\+ Start from scratch**.

![](https://files.readme.io/0adfba9-jm-start-from-scratch.png)

### 

Choose a template

The journey maps feature comes with a list of prebuilt templates based on specific use cases.

![](https://files.readme.io/2517c13-jm-choose-a-template.png)

Hover over a template to read a description of the journey and preview it by clicking the eye icon. If you're ready to start editing with the template, click **Use this template**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d99c3ea-jm-template-actions.png)

* * *

## 

2\. Configure basic settings

Fill out the basic settings of the journey and click **Continue**.

  * **Journey map name** : Enter a name for the journey map.
  * **Conversion event** : Add up to six events to track as conversions. Users are exited from the journey after completing a conversion event.
  * **Exit rule** : Add up to six exit events. Users are exited from the journey after completing the exit event.

![](https://files.readme.io/bafeddce467e1fb8ea712da033fde91b5af7e30e34a254fc4fbc98818fed2792-conversions-and-exit-rule.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Exiting the journey
> 
> Users exit the journey in one of three ways:
> 
>   1. They reached an exit node on the map.
>   2. They completed the conversion event.
>   3. They're dropped because they're not reachable.
> 


Next, proceed to [add nodes](doc:add-journey-nodes) to your journey.

  


__Updated 7 months ago

* * *

[Create a Journey Map](/docs/creating-a-journey-map)[Journey Copilot](/docs/journey-copilot)

# Channels

Integrate BotBonnie with your messaging channels allows you to better engage your users with less effort. Using BotBonnie, you can:

  * Automate customer services with chatbots and [AI rules](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/ai-rules) or auto-replies, and seamlessly hand off to live agents when needed.
  * Engage users with prebuilt templates and gamification kits to boost campaign participation.
  * Manage and respond to chats from multiple channels with [live chat](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/live-chat).



* * *

Supported channels

# Instagram

To connect an Instagram account with BotBonnie:

  * Ensure that you have one of the following types of Instagram accounts:
    * An Instagram Professional account for a business, or
    * An Instagram Professional account for a creator with fewer than 500k followers.
  * Link your Instagram account to a Facebook Page.
    * If you don’t have a Facebook Page, [create one](https://www.facebook.com/pages/create).
    * Ensure you have admin access to the Facebook Page.



Follow these steps to connect your Instagram account to BotBonnie.

* * *

## 

Part 1. Switch to Instagram professional account and connect to Facebook Page

The steps are slightly different between the mobile app version and the desktop version of Instagram.

  * Mobile version
  * Desktop version



### 

Mobile version

Follow the instructions below if you are setting up from a mobile device.

  * If your Instagram account is already a professional account, skip to step 3 to connect it to a Facebook Page.
  * If you are using an Instagram personal account, start from step 1 below to switch to a professional business account.


  1. In your Instagram account, click the menu icon in the top-right corner, click **Settings > Account**, and click **Switch to professional account**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6112e47-IG_professional_account_mobile.png)

  2. Click **Continue** multiple times to go through Instagram's feature introduction, select a category that describes your business, select **Business** , and click **Next**.

![](https://files.readme.io/bd30f7b-IG_business_mobile.png)

  3. Now you need to connect the Instagram account to your Facebook Page. You need to log into a Facebook account that has administrator access to the Facebook Page.
     * If you are continuing from step 2, click **Log into your Facebook** to log in, click **Continue** , select the Facebook Page and click **Next**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6775209-IG_connect_FB_Page_.png)

  * If your Instagram account is already a professional account and you just need to connect it to a Facebook Page, click **Edit profile** to go to your Instagram profile page. Click **Page** , click **Connect an existing Page** , select the Facebook Page you want to connect to, and click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/bf11c57-IG_connect_FB_Page.png)

You will see your Facebook Page listed on your Instagram profile page.

![](https://files.readme.io/ff550e2-IG_connected_FB.png)

Proceed to Part 2. Connect Instagram account to BotBonnie.

* * *

### 

Desktop version

Follow the instructions below if you are setting up from a desktop.

  * If your Instagram account is already a professional account, skip to step 4.
  * If you are using an Instagram personal account, start from step 1 below to switch to a professional business account.


  1. In your Instagram account, click **More** in the lower-left corner, click **Settings** , and click **Switch to professional account**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f8a1c03-Instagram_desktop_professional.png)

  2. Select **Business** , click **Next** to go through Instagram's feature introduction, select a category that describes your business, and click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f72b5de-Screen_Shot_2022-12-26_at_11.23.24_PM.png)

  3. Click **Done** to finish.

![](https://files.readme.io/0cb6a3a-IG_account_ready.png)

  4. Now you need to connect the Instagram account to your Facebook Page. Go to your Facebook Page, click the profile picture of your Facebook Page, and click **Settings & privacy**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4f25ff2-Screen_Shot_2022-12-23_at_6.27.28_PM.png)

  5. Select **Settings** and click **Linked Accounts**.

![](https://files.readme.io/b4cc6d6-Screen_Shot_2022-12-23_at_6.28.12_PM.png)

  6. Select **Instagram** and click **Connect Account**.

![](https://files.readme.io/dae7c03-Screen_Shot_2022-12-23_at_6.29.20_PM.png)

  7. Click **Connect**.

![](https://files.readme.io/06b2116-Screen_Shot_2022-12-23_at_6.29.51_PM.png)

  8. Make sure **Allow access to Instagram messages in Inbox** is enabled and click **Confirm**. This allows your Instagram account to send private messages to users who have commented.

![](https://files.readme.io/602d47f-Screen_Shot_2022-12-23_at_6.30.27_PM.png)

  9. Your Instagram account is now connected to a Facebook Page. Click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f2c51a8-IG_FB_Connected.png)

Proceed to Part 2 below.

* * *

## 

Part 2. Connect Instagram account to BotBonnie

  1. Go to the app launcher on the top-right, then click **Common settings**. Go to **Channels** , then click the **Instagram** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/66dcd93d2785b3b3648be1dbb87f659b54d3212c8db4c7abd6e9b6e2678b7dd0-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_1.png)

  2. Click **\+ Connect Instagram Business** to start the connection flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/f1459d8b9eb41aea754be03609be3341b47c3bd62626d124e5aaf281ca8bbf29-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_2.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Make sure you are logged in using a Facebook account that has administrator access to the Facebook Page you used for connection in part 1.

  3. Select the Instagram account to connect with BotBonnie and click **Next**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9a05f65-IG_-_Select_IG_business_account.png)

  4. Select the Facebook Page that is associated with the Instagram account and click **Next**.

![](https://files.readme.io/13cf86c-IG_-_Select_FB_page.png)

  5. Grant all permissions to BotBonnie and click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/b3b1c75-IG_-_grant_BB_access.png)

  6. Click **OK** to return to the enterprise console.

![](https://files.readme.io/137cca2-IG_-_finish_connecting.png)

  7. On the enterprise console, click the verticle three dots next to your Instagram account then select **Connect** to finish the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/7f1888c628907b503359edcd9426f9f894297a02218159793af8b6456bf2b876-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_3.png)

If your account was successfully connected, you'll see a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/19aed732a018d88faa5f0a42bfe1afc7433892b109cbf3e04a74bd0011de568c-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_4.png)

The connection status will be displayed on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/f644171e5a5873585e827bab573e33f15b43e8417d368123684df3c280d669d8-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_5.png)

* * *

## 

Test your connection

  1. From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/fbee7f72803e6390b7dde2127c0ae37b0ae3903a2743774cd578279f3cc9788b-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_2_1.png)

Send any message to your Instagram professional account and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/5fb9e00-Screen_Shot_2022-12-26_at_11.30.50_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Troubleshoot

If your Instagram account is not successfully connected to the enterprise console or your bot is not working on Instagram, here are some possible reasons.

### 

Your Instagram account is not properly connected to your Facebook Page

#### 

Mobile

  * On the Instagram app, click **Edit profile** and check if your Instagram account is connected to the Facebook Page. If not, follow the mobile instructions starting from step 3.

![](https://files.readme.io/a3616be-Screen_Shot_2022-12-26_at_11.18.57_AM.png)

  * In your Instagram account, click the menu icon in the top-right corner, click **Settings > Privacy > Messages**, and make sure **Allow access to messages** is enabled.

![](https://files.readme.io/4f68303-Screen_Shot_2022-12-26_at_6.15.34_PM.png)

#### 

Desktop

  * Log in to your Facebook Page, check if your Instagram account is connected and make sure **Allow access to Instagram messages in Inbox** is enabled. If not, follow the desktop instructions starting from step 4.

![](https://files.readme.io/c44048f-Screen_Shot_2022-12-26_at_5.50.16_PM.png)

### 

Your Instagram account is not connected to the BotBonnie bot

On the enterprise console, go to **Channels > Instagram**.

  * Click **Reset permissions** and re-grant access to the enterprise console.
  * If the Instagram account is connected to another bot, you can click **Connect** to connect it to the current bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/779f45b066a2d3d89b6a49d5c44fa37c56b986e778dc582b149af7115acec217-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_6_1.png)

If your issues cannot be resolved, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

# KakaoTalk

Integrating your KakaoTalk channel with BotBonnie allows you to manage Kakao Moment and Sangdam Talk with BotBonnie.

  * **Kakao Moment** : Kakao Moment messages are one-way promotional messages (for example, annual sale announcement) that can be sent to users who opted in. After integration, you can use BotBonnie's [broadcast](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/broadcasting-create-broadcasts) feature to send Kakao Moment messages.
  * **Sangdam Talk** : Sangdam Talk messages are 1-on-1 conversations initiated by users. After integration, you can use BotBonnie's chat-related features to manage Sangdam Talk messages.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Alim Talk (transactional messages) is not supported by BotBonnie.
>   * To comply with regulations in Korea, promotional messages can only be sent to users between 8:00 AM - 9:00 PM (Korea Standard Time).
> 


To connect a KakaoTalk channel with BotBonnie:

  * Create a KakaoTalk channel if you do not have a KakaoTalk channel yet.
  * You need to have access to the KakaoTalk Channel Admin Center of your channel.



* * *

## 

Set up your KakaoTalk channels

### 

Part 1. Switch to a business KakaoTalk channel

If you already have a business KakaoTalk channel, skip to Part 2. Configure channel settings.

If your KakaoTalk channel is not a business channel, log in to [Kakao Talk Channel Admin Center](https://center-pf.kakao.com/), go to **Channel (채널) > Management (관리) > Apply Business Channel (비즈니스 채널 신청)** and click **Application (신청)**.

![](https://files.readme.io/98866cc-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.50.32_PM.png)

You will need to submit the following certificates to Kakao and wait 3 to 5 business days for Kakao's approval.

  * Certificate of Business Registration
  * Certificate of Employment or ID card of Representative
  * Registration certificates (only for a mail-order business, medical device sales, and health functional food sales business)



For more details, see [Kakao's official guide](https://t1.daumcdn.net/biz/DM/%EB%B9%84%EC%A6%88%EB%8B%88%EC%8A%A4%20%EC%B1%84%EB%84%90%20%EC%8B%A0%EC%B2%AD%EA%B0%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C_ver1.0_%EC%99%B8%EB%B6%80%EC%9A%A9.pdf) (available in Korean only).

### 

Part 2. Configure channel settings

In the Kakao Talk Channel Admin Center, go to **Channel (채널) > Dashboard (대시보드)** and make sure the following options are enabled.

  * **Public channel (채널 공개)**
  * **Allow search (검색 허용)**
  * **1:1 chat (1:1채팅)**

![](https://files.readme.io/8ddf499-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.52.37_PM.png)

### 

Part 3. Create a Kakao Talk developer channel (recommended)

You can create a Kakao Talk developer channel for testing. A developer channel allows you to test BotBonnie features without affecting your channel users and avoid service fees.

You need to have a business KakaoTalk channel in order to create a developer channel.

In your KakaoTalk Channel Admin Center, go to **Channel (채널)** , click **Create a channel for developers (개발자용 채널 만들기)** , and create a test channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/b1334f6-Screen_Shot_2023-07-11_at_9.26.35_PM.png)

Go to **Channel (채널) > Dashboard (대시보드)** and make sure **Public channel (채널 공개)** is enabled.

![](https://files.readme.io/7616cc7-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.55.03_PM.png)

### 

Part 4. Provide channel information to your CSM

Contact your customer success manager and provide the following channel information.

  * **KakaoTalk channel** (your production environment)
    * **Channel name (채널 이름)**
    * **Search ID (검색용 아이디)**
  * **KakaoTalk developer channel** (if available)
    * **Channel name (채널 이름)**



You can find this information by going to **Channel (채널) > Management (관리) > Detailed settings (상세설정)** in KakaoTalk Channel Admin Center.

![](https://files.readme.io/9c77682-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_7.01.09_PM.png)

Next, BotBonnie team will complete the rest of the account integration and notify you when the connection is ready.

* * *

## 

Check the connected channels

After BotBonnie team completes the integration, you can now check the connected channels on the console.

  1. Go to the app launcher on the top-right, then click **Common settings**. Go to **Channels** on the sidebar, then click the **KakaoTalk** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/4cd449b158f1ff38606cf60d8e536b455b6dafccdd63ce69c7d7abc6ca9251fd-Kakao.png)

  2. You will see your connected channels with a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/777aa1c918c3076cd069cde40f4fda1ced566860a6c568631aa851f212a81045-Connecting_an_KakaoTalk_Channel_1.png)

* * *

## 

Test your connection

From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/28c6867853d7c68823a1e79eb86ef8d9ad82cf39c1b807fd4fa590abf6db1b07-AI_rules_2.png)

Expand the new AI rule, enter a keyword under **User input** (for example, `test`), select **Exact match** , select a module to be the **Bot reply** , and click **Deploy bot**. The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to your KakaoTalk channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/89ebaa7-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.07.02_PM.png)

Send a message to your KakaoTalk channel that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

# LINE

Connect your LINE Official Account (OA) to BotBonnie to engage with users through automated chatbot interactions. This integration allows you to automate responses, manage conversations, and enhance customer engagement on LINE.

To connect your LINE OA:

  * Ensure you have admin access to your [LINE OA](https://manager.line.biz/) and [LINE Developers console](https://developers.line.biz/console/).
  * Your Messaging API and LINE Login channels must be under the same LINE provider.



You can also [enable live agent chatroom](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/channels-line#enable-live-agent-chatroom-for-line) to seamlessly switch between automated and human customer agent support.

* * *

## 

Connect a LINE OA to BotBonnie

  1. Go to the app launcher on the top-right, then click **Common settings**. Go to **Channels** , then click the **LINE** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/84e572f0d67295da9a32186bba00a6350a062b3a261f092c0856bde78ee37d60-LINE.png)

  2. Click **\+ Connect LINE OA** to start the connection flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/de876cffe9938bcee582dca46387410432cb07dc8a9934d7bf7e4a11120e309a-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_2.png)

### 

Part I. Connect Messaging API channel

  1. Log in to [LINE Developers Console](https://developers.line.biz/console/), go to the provider of your LINE OA, and check if there's already a Messaging API channel.

     1. If there's no Messaging API channel under the provider, see how to create a Messaging API channel.
     2. If a Messaging API channel exists and you have admin access, return to BotBonnie's console and click **Continue** to complete **① Create Messaging API channel** and **② Enable Messaging API**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e9d41f8-LINE_channels_API.png)

  2. On LINE Developers console, click the Messaging API channel and go to the **Basic settings** tab.

     1. Find **Channel ID** , click the copy icon, and paste the **Channel ID** on BotBonnie console under **1\. Connect a Messaging API channel > ③ Set up channel ID**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/b92f6ad7988606ced8db68e10794028b848e58b3055e6ee7c671bcb88786b7df-line-developer-channel-id.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/ac4434c0a5d2c6f355164618a4beb238006f9c40abf0a58abe9b2cbadea36cb0-channel-id-bb.png)

ii. Copy the **Channel secret** from LINE Developers console, paste it on BotBonnie console under **1\. Connect a Messaging API channel > ④ Set up channel secret**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/1aa14e18b16b0ec96f62862caf18fbdd73dadb11de61ce9525620ff53ed07ef6-line-developer-channel-secret.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/3c28dda4eb5c0dd79e24434700596c61e457586a6e61594f9e5ae9510c2525b4-channel-secret-bb.png)

  3. On LINE Developers console, go to the **Messaging API** tab in the Messaging API channel.
     1. Find the **Channel access token** , click **Issue** if empty, copy the channel access token, paste it on BotBonnie console under **1\. Connect a Messaging API channel > ⑤ Set up channel access token**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/7abca7804ad4adb7c2d3884e1046a8f716812986cdbc976f556b6db4f58f704e-access-token-bb.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/f627d50975588d2abfcdb4f8690c77df27ae8c10dda081744ec532e9960fd6dd-channel-access-token-bb.png)

  4. Reload the LINE Developers console. Go to **Messaging API** > **Webhook settings** , and enable **Use webhook** , followed by **Webhook redelivery** and **Error statistic aggregation**.

![](https://files.readme.io/994dddd1b813200fdfefbcedfdffdef1817605ed21d14b5f8c891e898a727531-webhook-enabled.png)

  5. Go back to BotBonnie console, click **Continue** under **⑥ Set up Webhook**.
  6. Log in to [LINE Official Account Manager](https://manager.line.biz/), select the LINE OA you're configuring, click **Settings** > **Response settings**.
     1. Disable **Chat** , **Greeting message** , and **Auto-response messages**.
     2. Make sure **Webhooks** is enabled.

![](https://files.readme.io/8a212a8-Screen_Shot_2023-04-07_at_6.58.14_PM.png)

  7. Return to the BotBonnie console. Click **Connect login channel** to complete the LINE login channel setup.

![](https://files.readme.io/1e28fe570ccb53e7dd76ab57aeae292948511e4fd28949cfe94ff702c2c315c3-step7-LINEOA-settings.png)

### 

Part II. Connect LINE Login channel

  8. On LINE Developers console, go to the same provider as your Messaging API channel and check if there's already a LINE Login channel.


  * If you don't see a LINE Login channel under the same provider with the Messaging API channel, see how to create a LINE Login channel.
  * If there's a LINE Login channel, make sure you have admin access to the channel. You can skip **2\. Connect a Login channel > ① Create Login channel** on BotBonnie console.

![](https://files.readme.io/700367b-LINE_channels_Login.png)

  9. On LINE Developers console, click the LINE Login channel and go to the **Basic settings** tab.
     1. Find the **Channel ID** , copy the channel ID, paste it on BotBonnie console under **2\. Connect a Login channel > ② Set up channel ID**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/77953c28a39c6e85a966f91875757a9e3501ca628e63a81a863e55710ff8694c-login-channel-id.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/6d5f038426378be7c0f39f88f99609f142764412d074bc016be8a1180e1e10ed-login-channel-id-bb.png)

ii. Find **Channel secret** , copy the **Channel secret** , paste it on BotBonnie console under **2\. Connect a Login channel > ③ Set up channel secret**, and click **Complete**.

![](https://files.readme.io/003fd4dd36708161b6f162fbe2c1a83128265c279f32ab1d49f2a09cdfe2b0ee-login-channel-secret.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/9c578b6a41f3c8e3762439fdf0018d6741409ebdbdfc3673073e891570af935d-login-channel-bb.png)

  10. If the login channel is not published yet, scroll up to the top of the LINE Login channel, click **Developing** and click **Publish**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ab3d914-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_8.54.44_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/1f65266-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_8.55.20_AM.png)

If your account was successfully connected, you'll see a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/138bd47baba1052b50e5c3fab2b7f6dfd1c6fbfc783bfe5d0042f80c572a10dd-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_3_1.png)

The connection status will be displayed on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/e5d55d0fbc66dca9cc1b2b3bcbac22ef6dc9cc48ed8103464bdd1ab2f0e29be5-line-card-connected.png)

### 

Test your connection

  1. From the left menu, go to **Content** > **AI rules** , then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5e7f92ef67597d2d6d459ab5c015d855f067e271137be4a66eb292b1efe1d5c1-AI_rules.png)

  2. Expand the new AI rule and complete the following settings.
     1. Enter a keyword under **User input** (such as `test`). The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to your LINE OA.
     2. Under **Condition** , select **Exact match**.
     3. Under **Bot reply** , select an advanced kit, such as a lucky wheel, daily check-in, or scratch-off kit.
     4. Click **Deploy bot**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4ffbe52-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_7.41.48_PM.png)

  3. Send a message to your LINE OA that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the advanced kit you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/36b112e-Screen_Shot_2023-04-11_at_2.16.20_PM.png)

  4. Run the advanced kit to make sure it works.

![](https://files.readme.io/b119efd-Screen_Shot_2023-04-11_at_2.18.41_PM.png)

If errors occur while running the advanced kit, the LINE Login channel might not be connected properly. See how to troubleshoot.

### 

Troubleshooting

Here are some common issues you might run into while setting up your LINE OA with BotBonnie:

  * I can't find the Messaging API Channel
  * I can't find the LINE Login Channel
  * I can't click the Complete button on BotBonnie console
  * Your LINE OA is connected to another BotBonnie bot
  * Advanced kits and growth tools are not working properly



#### 

I can't find the Messaging API Channel

If you cannot find the Messaging API channel under the provider on LINE Official Account Manager, log into [LINE Developers Console](https://developers.line.biz/console/), and go to **Settings > Messaging API** to check if a Messaging API channel is already created for the LINE OA.

![](https://files.readme.io/38baa58-LINEOA_Settings_MsgAPI.png)

If the Messaging API channel is enabled already but not visible on LINE Developers console, it's possible that you do not have access to it. Check with your team to request access to the Messaging API channel. To see how to grant channel access, refer to [LINE's documentation](https://developers.line.biz/en/docs/line-developers-console/managing-roles/#role-settings-for-channel).

![](https://files.readme.io/9fdcece-LINEOA_MsgAPI_Enabled.png)

If Messaging API is not enabled, click **Enable Messaging API** and select the provider of the LINE OA to enable. This creates a Messaging API channel for your LINE OA.

![](https://files.readme.io/5e466c0-LINEOA_MessagingAPI.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/a726014-LINEOA_MsgAPI_Enabled.png)

#### 

I can't find the LINE Login Channel

If you cannot find the LINE Login channel under the provider on LINE Developers console, click **Create a new channel** and select **LINE Login** to create a login channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/b9fd6eb-LINE_newchannel.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/aae9766-LINE_newlogin.png)

Set **App types** to **Web app** and complete all other settings.

![](https://files.readme.io/8ae6865-Screen_Shot_2023-04-07_at_7.23.44_PM.png)

Click **Create**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d5aa442-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_9.44.10_AM.png)

#### 

I can't click the Complete button on BotBonnie console

If you have reached the final step on BotBonnie console and the **Complete** button is grayed out, this means that some steps are not completed yet.

![](https://files.readme.io/25eb35c-line-complete-button.png)

Make sure both parts are marked as 100% completed.

![](https://files.readme.io/23cbe66-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_11.18.05_AM.png)

Each step should have a green checkmark next to it. If not, go back to that step, make sure the field is entered correctly and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/33a8ebee82d168de236c7e825578f8bcc10e63e259df111664ee0d81abed5c33-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_-_I_cant_click_the_Complete_button_on_BotBonnie_Console.png)

#### 

Your LINE OA is connected to another BotBonnie bot

If the LINE OA is connected to another bot, you can click the vertical three dots next to the LINE OA and select **Connect** to connect it to the current bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/26538c4b251d267f2fea6245ca899da59f9f1562bac25c26141b39ff98148865-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_5.png)

#### 

Advanced kits and growth tools are not working properly

If your advanced kits, growth tools, or referral links are not working, the most common cause is that the Messaging API and LINE Login channels are set up under different providers, which prevents the same user ID from being assigned to the same user.

To check this, go to your [LINE Developers Console](https://developers.line.biz/console/) and verify that both the Messaging API channel and LINE Login channel are listed under the same provider.

![](https://files.readme.io/8693a4c-LINE_channels_provider.png)

Next, make sure the channel ID and channel secret for the LINE Login channel are entered correctly in BotBonnie. Go to **Channels** > **LINE** , click the vertical three dots next to the connected LINE OA, and select **Settings**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c71830dceae9103eaaf2ac1a8b6bf143a7301d7fc1c4afc03d59fe7a093bf669-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_6.png)

If your channels are under different providers, create a new channel under the provider that's already associated with your connected LINE OA.

If your issues cannot be resolved, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

* * *

## 

Integrate with other Appier products

You can integrate your LINE Official Account in BotBonnie with your other Appier products like AIQUA and AiDeal to enhance your engagement strategies. To set up any integration, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

After the integration is completed, you can manage your integration settings by going to **Channels** > **LINE** , clicking the vertical three dots next to the connected LINE OA, and clicking **App Integration**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5c0015eec44274f619d3f15ef6978860b535a43767409a0ebf00c638674e6990-app-integration.png)

* * *

## 

Enable live agent chatroom for LINE

The BotBonnie [live agent chatroom](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/live-chat#live-agent-chatroom) lets you manage one-on-one user interactions on LINE without incurring messaging fees. When users enter the chatroom, it opens in a LIFF web view within the LINE app.

Follow the steps below to enable this feature:

  1. Go to **Channels** on the sidebar and click the **LINE** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/d3b987b585013be094ee7483e6ef4d10d2409d7b4b02d567d7c5416240bab8c7-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_12.png)

  2. Click the vertical three dots next to the LINE OA and select **Settings**.

![](https://files.readme.io/11b46e8ea97e4693b0490dbf9b0dc1d6bc4e451703f804e96a7b70830d92cbe7-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_8.png)

  3. Next, click on **Live agent chatroom** tab, toggle the **Live agent chatroom** switch to allow your live agent to chat with users.

![](https://files.readme.io/c02222c8a8b1d71fb8a8cfeafadb5432589ffc556cd736c5f2e48d59186d5237-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_9.png)

  4. Enter a chatroom name to appear at the top of the chatroom.
  5. Enable the **Chatroom announcement** to display a message at the top of conversation when needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/5db5b3371aa6cbec4e85953489e81dbd407c2f39c3180dc7ce83727064b8de75-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_10.png)

  6. Turn on the **Unread message reminder** and set it up to remind users when they haven't read a message for a specified period of time.
  7. Set the reminder period and select a time unit (minutes, hours, or days) from the dropdown.
  8. Configure the **Reminder module** to define the message users receive when reminded.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The unread message reminder is sent via a LINE OA and may incur LINE messaging fees. Customer service agents can [manage the unread message reminder](doc:live-chat#managing-unread-messages-in-your-chatroom) for each live chat chatroom as needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/68b76455b5935698aff7bde76cb361f67e0991634642165efc8295bf6a88af44-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_11.png)

After enabling this feature, you can add the button to [**chatbot flows**](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/triggered-actions#chatbot-flows-buttons-quick-replies-and-user-input) by selecting **Open live agent chatroom** on click action and initiating live agent chatroom within live chat.

__Updated 6 months ago

# Messenger

Connect your Messenger to BotBonnie to engage with your audience using automated chatbot interactions. This integration allows you to automate responses, manage interactions, and optimize your marketing efforts on Messenger.

Before connecting your Messenger account:

  1. [Create a Facebook Page](https://www.facebook.com/pages/create) if you don’t already have one.
  2. Ensure you have admin access to your Facebook Page.



You can also integrate the Meta Conversion API to enhance tracking and optimize campaigns with detailed user interactions.

* * *

## 

Connect a Messenger account

Follow the instructions below to connect your Messenger account to BotBonnie.

  1. Go to the app launcher on the top-right, then click **Common settings**. Go to **Channels** , then click the **Messenger** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/e20997b04884c1765c893c82ea9fdc237e863e546209a92305fd2ce96b3aef4d-Facebook.png)

  2. Click **\+ Connect Messenger** to start the connection flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/267d6c2b2b1fde381e603a668ec36d32bda66758b601c4e1dc12489f148c0719-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_2.png)

  3. Log in using a Facebook account that has admin access or full control of the Facebook Page associated with the Messenger account you want to connect.
  4. Select the Facebook Page linked to the Messenger account you want to connect with BotBonnie and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/73a994ce97bb15cf4879644bef434c78e20339c292fdcf532a79b81934678022-Frame_1321317055.png)

  5. Select the Businesses you want to connect to BotBonnie and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/960d4cca214945617d765bf9a06dbe6b8e29a3b948f16321710d65ac7cada258-Frame_1321317057.png)

  6. On the enterprise console, click the vertical three dots next to your messenger account, then select **Connect** to finish the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1a9581a79360b1a7376798bf34f7c791621598152363dab8e106dad87b8f80a-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_3.png)

If your account was successfully connected, you'll see a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/dd3f413dc1a207b7c400d563c5c3f4be0eef63ba2d3923c563f9c9e03293ffc7-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_4.png)

The connection status will be displayed on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/6f1d8dac0efe32b527c4cc59d61c6e0c1af9cb204bc81c6e814374258a9322bf-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_5.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> For advanced data tracking, [Enable the Meta Conversion API](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/channels-messenger#enable-the-meta-conversion-api-integration) to send event data to Meta and enhance your tracking and advertising capabilities.

### 

Test your connection

From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/fba66d47702993df92c845ba99c84cb800d41895f3e15eb1ed58dd4ec85a4c5e-ai-rule.png)

Expand the new AI rule, enter a keyword under **User input** (for example, `test`), select **Exact match** , select a module to be the **Bot reply** , and click **Deploy bot**. The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to your Messenger account.

![](https://files.readme.io/4ef59e9-BB_NewRule.png)

Send a message to your Messenger account that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/b96cf25-BB_FBTestResults.png)

### 

Troubleshooting

If your Messenger account is not successfully connected to BotBonnie or your bot is not working on the Messenger account, here are some possible reasons.

  * Third-party cookies are blocked on your browser
  * Your Facebook account needs full control or admin access of the associated Facebook Page
  * Your Messenger account is not properly connected to the BotBonnie bot



#### 

Third-party cookies are blocked on your browser

Check the settings of your browser to make sure third-party cookies are not blocked. We'll use Chrome as an example.

Open Chrome, click the menu button in the top-right corner, and go to **Settings > Privacy and security > Cookies and other site data**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e8fc23c-BB_BrowserSetting.png)

Make sure third-party cookies are not blocked.

![](https://files.readme.io/fe298c0-BB_BrowserCookies.png)

#### 

Your Facebook account needs full control or admin access of the associated Facebook Page

If you're using the New Pages Experiences, your Facebook account needs to have full control of the Facebook Page.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d88ba3-BB_FBFullAccess.png)

If you're using Classic Pages, your Facebook account needs to be an Admin of the Facebook Page.

![](https://files.readme.io/074b2f2-BB_FBAdminAccess.png)

Here's how Classic Page roles compare to the access levels in the new Pages experience:

Classic Page roles| Page access in the New Pages Experience  
---|---  
Admin| Facebook access with full control  
Editor| Facebook access with partial control  
Moderator| Task access for Message Replies, Community Activity, Ads, Insights  
Advertiser| Task access for Ads, Insights  
Analyst| Task access for Insights  
Community manager| Community manager access to moderate Live chats  
  
To see how to check the access level of your Facebook account, refer to [Facebook Help Center](https://www.facebook.com/help/510247025775149/).

#### 

Your Messenger account is not properly connected to the BotBonnie bot

On the enterprise console, go to **Channels** > **Messenger**.

  * Click **Reset permissions** and re-grant access to BotBonnie.

![](https://files.readme.io/187381e242299a0b08c8e8c7a932b1023eaeee593898c79c38665122647f3952-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_6.png)

  * If the Messenger account is connected to another bot, you can click **Connect** to connect it to the current bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/2ad7965965da481f33c81b01a246aa6f747ece7108a819bf7291ad1dde5f314a-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_7.png)

If your issues cannot be resolved, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

* * *

## 

Enable the Meta Conversion API integration

The Meta Conversion API allows you to track key customer events in Messenger, such as adding products to cart, purchasing, initiating checkouts, and submitting leads for better advertising campaign optimization.

Ensure you have full access permissions to enable the integration for the Messenger account you want to connect.

  1. If your bot isn't yet connected to a Messenger account, click **\+ Connect Messenger** to connect a Messenger account.

![](https://files.readme.io/5c5ea567180fa4b2c9cd48bc877396144b338096fe90f7432dae217437cdc99e-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_8.png)

  2. For the Messenger account you'd like to enable the Conversion API for, click the vertical three dots, then select **Connect Meta Conversion API**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f0e3844a89fa0f99e0be1f929a106050902d1b5cc7f101811b193abc7c3f8dfc-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_9.png)

  3. You'll be redirected to Meta's authorization page.

     1. Select the appropriate business account and Facebook page associated with your Messenger account.
     2. In the final step, make sure you see the "Log events on your Page's behalf and send those events to Facebook" and click **Save**.
![](https://files.readme.io/b6ae5e0337d86bbd8aa6b3615c71e220c683fc1bf7c1cedaac0cd20a096f6646-authentication.png)



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Skip the Instagram and WhatsApp account selections, as they are not supported for this integration.

  4. After completing the connection, check the BotBonnie console to verify the connection status:


  * **Connected (Conversion API)** indicates a successful connection.
  * **Error** suggests a connection error.

![](https://files.readme.io/7f90e5c0e3ac46ce8a0f347467d7da1041b8f5b24d332597d8f49494f82eaee2-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_10.png)

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Troubleshooting Conversion API connection error
> 
> If you encounter an error while connecting your bot with the Meta Conversion API, check that:
> 
>   * You have full page access.
>   * A dataset has been created in Meta Business Suite. To learn more, see [Create a dataset during messaging event set creation](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/5818684664831465?id=490360542427371).
> 


Once the Conversion API integration is complete, you'll be able to [send conversion events to Meta using triggered actions in buttons](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/triggered-actions#send-conversion-event-to-meta).

# Viber

Integrate your bot with Viber to send [broadcasts](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts) to your users using their phone number. To learn how to get started, contact your customer success manager.

* * *

## 

Send Viber broadcast

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> See [Broadcasts](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts) to how to create and send broadcasts.

### 

Supported message types

The following message types are supported for Viber broadcasts:

  * **Text (Viber)** : Consists of a text-only message. Can contain one button.
  * **Image (Viber)** : Consists of a title and an image. Can contain one button.

![](https://files.readme.io/b701a5999a4c94061f2697cdc67d23a1e1ad6c47908f32832d38fded7b8321a2-viber-message-types.png)

### 

Phone number requirements

  * Broadcasts can only be sent to users who have a phone number saved to the **Mobile** field in their profile. Users in the broadcast segment without a saved phone number won't receive the broadcast.
  * To avoid delivery issues, phone numbers should include the country code, including the leading `+`.

![](https://files.readme.io/b98440eba0b96638a24f7891d696e81f64ef0195746b10db158b4cd3d4b68c2d-user-profile-mobile-number.png)

# WebChat

WebChat is BotBonnie's chat widget that can be installed on your website. Using WebChat, you can:

  * Utilize BotBonnie features to automate customer services
  * Customize the look and feel of your WebChat widget
  * Control the web pages where you want the WebChat to be displayed
  * Let users link to their accounts on other messaging platforms

![](https://files.readme.io/0edc70f-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_7.52.39_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Set up WebChat

  1. Go to the app launcher on the top-right, then click **Common settings**. Go to **Channels** , then click the **WebChat** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/ebf0179f4b82c5e3854397e45c567d31a6fd5e698f774d9be8c4d427e7a5d9f5-Webchat.png)

  2. Click **Set up** to start the connection flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/d65b4c9d8d77447dc976877bbc721cef66e68cb630b177602d36cb5ebd8435b6-WebChat_integration_2.png)

You will need to set up the following sections on the console, and then install the WebChat code to your website.

  * Step 1. Basic info
  * Step 2. Website URL
  * Step 3. Customer service slots
  * Step 4. Messaging platform linking
  * Step 5. Installation



### 

Step 1. Basic info

#### 

Basic information

  * **Brand name** : Type the name of your business. This is the name that will be displayed to the user.
  * **Welcome message (optional)** : The welcome message is displayed at the top of the WebChat home page.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you have selected a Getting Started module in **Content** > **Greetings** , this greeting message will be displayed before the user starts a conversation with you.

![](https://files.readme.io/d9fc08d-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.29.55_PM.png)

#### 

WebChat widget settings

  * **Brand logo** : Click **Upload image** and upload a logo image in JPG / PNG format that's less than 2 MB.
  * **Brand color** : Choose the theme color of your WebChat.

![](https://files.readme.io/04547ec-webchat-widget-settings.png)

#### 

Launcher settings

Launcher is the chat icon that will be displayed on your website.

  * **Launcher preview** : When you are setting up the launcher, you can enter the URL of your website and click Preview to see what the launcher will look like on the website. Depending on the URL you input, it will take up to 3~20 seconds to load your website.
  * **Launcher image** : Click **Upload image** and upload an image that will be the chat icon shown to users.
  * **Launcher position** : Select to align the launcher to the bottom-left or bottom-right corner of the browser window.
  * **Launcher margins** : Set the spacing between your launcher and the edges of the browser window. You can set different margins for mobile devices. Depending on the **Launcher position** selected above, the bottom-left or bottom-right edges will be used for alignment.

![](https://files.readme.io/b7b0967-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.26.53_PM.png)

#### 

Chat window settings

  * **Enable input fields in the chat window** : If no agent is available, you can choose to hide input fields in the chat window.
  * **Skip the homepage and open the chat window directly** : When enabled, the homepage will be hidden. Users will not be able to see the chat options for the social platforms set up under **Messaging platform linking**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ee14772-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.29.14_PM.png)

When done, click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 2. Website URL

You can control whether you want to display the WebChat widget on every page of the website or only on certain pages.

  * **Show the WebChat widget on every page** : The WebChat widget will be shown on every webpage with the WebChat snippet installed.
  * **Only show the WebChat widget on pages matching your URL rules** : The WebChat widget will be shown on web pages that match any of the URL rules you set. Click **Add URL rule** , select one of the operators below, and enter a URL (or part of a URL).
    * `is`: The widget will be shown if the webpage URL is an exact match of what you have entered.
    * `contains`: The widget will be shown if the webpage URL contains what you have entered.

![](https://files.readme.io/ffdc6997450902ddf4f37ab56c364e3e7cebebb5cc143a8fff8aa788b9530b71-WebChat_integration_3.png)

When done, click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 3. Customer service slots

The business hours and agent off-hours configurations in the Settings page are applied to WebChat. Make sure they are set up properly.

  * **Business hours** : To set up the business hours, click **Go edit** and you will be taken to the **Settings > Business hours** page. During business hours, an **Online** icon will be displayed in the WebChat. Outside of business hours, an **Offline** icon will be displayed.
  * **Agent off-hours** : To set up agent off-hours and autoreply, click **Go edit** and you'll be taken to the **Settings > Notify agent** page. If enabled, when the user triggers the **Notify agent** outside of business hours:
    * An autoreply will be sent to remind your users that agents will respond during business hours.
    * Notifications through LINE Notify will be paused, while email notifications will still be sent.

![](https://files.readme.io/f85f571-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.04.59_PM.png)

When done, click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 4. Messaging platform linking

You can let users contact you via other messaging platforms such as Facebook Messenger, LINE, and WhatsApp. By doing so, the users’ browsers will be linked with their accounts on the messaging platforms, allowing you to identify the same user across different platforms.

When users click on the messaging platform in WebChat, they will be directed to send a message to your channel on that platform. Upon successful linking, a success message will be sent to the users.

![](https://files.readme.io/ab32c5d-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_6.50.34_PM.png)

To set up, select the messaging platforms you want to enable and complete the following settings.

  * **Description** : Enter a short sentence to let users know that they can chat with you through this platform. You can also provide incentives to encourage users to complete linking.
  * **Channel** : Select the channel to link to.
  * **Message of successful linking** : Set the module that will be sent to the users upon successful linking. You can use this to introduce the services available on the platform or to provide the incentives you have offered to users.
  * **Triggered actions** : Select any actions you want to trigger upon successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/9ab93f2-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_7.00.08_PM.png)

Click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 5. Installation

Under the Installation tab, click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/2729d9e5b811ce6b2f7e3679b10fa78b0510f08e0923af1e85fc355676a5aac3-WebChat_integration_4.png)

You will see the page ID of the WebChat shown on the console. In the next section, you will use this page ID to install the WebChat code snippet to your website.

![](https://files.readme.io/505b1163b461d0aad221a6db74803f3bc7dc1fa2100d79489165549f92884ed1-WebChat_integration_5.png)

* * *

## 

Install the WebChat code

Now you need to install the WebChat code snippet to every page of your website. In the instructions below, we will use Google Tag Manager to complete the installation.

  1. Copy the page ID of your WebChat bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/a98d8793cf995df53fc57711cdec62be24646488aacd7c5c7f1c29dc59cf27d0-WebChat_integration_5.png)

  2. Use the copied page ID to replace the `<YOUR_WEBCHAT_PAGEID>` part in the code snippets below.



WebChat code
    
    
    <script>
      window.BBAsyncInit = function() {
        BB.init({
          appId: "<YOUR_WEBCHAT_PAGEID>"
        });
      };
    </script>
    <script async type="text/javascript" src="https://asset.botbonnie.com/sdk/sdk.js"></script>
    

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you are not using Google Tag Manager, skip the steps below and add the code snippet before the end of the `</body>` tag on every page of your website.

  3. Log into your Google Tag Manager account, click **Tags** , and click **New** to create a new tag.

![](https://files.readme.io/436e731-newtag.png)

  4. Rename the tag (for example, WebChat Tag), click the pencil icon in **Tag Configuration** , and select **Custom HTML**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1ab77c-edittag.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/23d251c-customHTML.png)

  5. Under HTML, paste the code snippet from step 2.



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Make sure you have replaced the page ID.

![](https://files.readme.io/f66c6b7777f2d4c3ceb7ac3459204fd7160e046cf77eb3eb094f5370f611d43a-WebChat_integration_-_code_snippet.png)

  6. Click the edit icon in **Triggering** , select **All Pages** , and click **Save**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f6e9dbe-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_9.46.33_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/a0f9a98-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_9.48.37_AM.png)

  7. Click **Preview** and enter your website URL.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1b5fe0-TagPreview.png)

Make sure the WebChat tag you created is firing properly.

![](https://files.readme.io/9a277ae-DebugMode.png)

  8. When you’re done checking, click **Submit > Publish**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6860725-TagPreview_copy.png)

You should see the WebChat widget on your website.

![](https://files.readme.io/27244a0-webchat_widget.png)

* * *

## 

Test your integration

From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c597df230e24cf2ae6c05a5e5ab5b4fd62c5d60dc5924d564eb4ede28081f270-AI_rules_2.png)

Expand the new AI rule, enter a keyword under **User input** (for example, `test`), select **Exact match** , select a module to be the **Bot reply** , and click **Deploy bot**. The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to you.

![](https://files.readme.io/89ebaa7-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.07.02_PM.png)

Send a message to your WebChat that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule, and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/3990fd1-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_7.14.03_PM.png)

__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

# WhatsApp

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Premium feature
> 
> Contact your customer success manager to learn more about this feature.

You can integrate your WhatsApp Business Account with your BotBonnie account to start automating customer services and broadcasting engaging content to your users. Currently, WhatsApp supports two types of messages:

  * Business-initiated messages: Can only be sent to users who have opted in and must use the [**Message template**](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/messaging-channel-whatsapp-manage-message-templates) created in Meta Business Manager and approved by Meta.
  * User-initiated messages: If a user initiates a chat and you reply within 24 hours, your replies can be free-format without requiring pre-approved templates. After 24 hours, replies are considered business-initiated messages and require pre-approved templates.



* * *

## 

Setup requirements

  1. [Create a Meta Business account](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/1710077379203657?id=180505742745347) if you don't already have one. Make sure you have admin access to your Meta Business account.
  2. Prepare a phone number for your WhatsApp Business Account:
     * If you already have a WhatsApp Business Account connected to another provider, fill out this [form](https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLScgd_70zC6OshJ7R0MseG1Q4Xkqs83BN8USzvoqotCRMkQhjQ/viewform) before proceeding.
     * Use a business phone number that isn't registered to WhatsApp yet or [rent a new number from Vonage](https://www.vonage.com/communications-apis/voice/pricing/).



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Your chosen number must meet these requirements:
> 
>   * Can receive SMS from the United States.
>   * Isn't already registered to a WhatsApp account.
> 


  3. Contact your customer support manager with your Meta business account details and phone number. They'll connect your WhatsApp Business Account through Vonage and complete the connection with BotBonnie.
  4. Once your account connects successfully, you'll see a **Connected** status with a green check icon on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/37c3f90c8eada5b9f5b58627243902bbf9020aa8bae03e38fedc76fa37cfc04d-whatsapp-connected.png)

* * *

## 

Test connection

To test your connection, you can create an [AI rule](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/ai-rules), then send a message containing the keyword you specified to your WhatsApp account. If your WhatsApp account was successfully connected, you'll receive the reply module you selected.

![](https://files.readme.io/6b45abf2d8348768d706f496c20dedf0ac54e6a214e2167a003c6bc94f300359-whatsapp-test-connection.png)

Once your connection is working, you can start engaging with your users. To send a business-initiated message using the [broadcast](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/broadcasting-create-broadcasts) feature, follow the instructions to [create a message template](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/channels-whatsapp-manage-message-templates).

# 91APP

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channel
> 
>   * LINE
> 


Connect your 91APP store to the BotBonnie chatbot to enable users who link their accounts to view their shopping information.

Follow these setup steps to add a connection:

  1. Obtain credentials
  2. Connect with 91APP
  3. (Optional) Set up account linking



After setup, you can [manage connections](doc:common-settings-integration-e-commerce-integrations#manage-connections) on whenever necessary.

* * *

## 

1\. Obtain credentials

To connect BotBonnie to the 91APP platform, contact the 91APP support team to request the following credentials:

  * API key (`x-api-key`)
  * Webhook shared secret (which includes a username and secret)



* * *

## 

2\. Connect with 91APP

Follow these steps to connect your 91APP store to BotBonnie.

  1. Open the project launcher in the top navigation bar, select **Common settings** , and then click **EC platforms** in the left navigation panel.

![](https://files.readme.io/42d9e57b124e8eaa22c136bae1bc157fed1c1f4b734c857cb4a8a5c4c63055d4-nav-ec-platforms.png)

  2. Click the **91APP** card to start the connection setup.

![](https://files.readme.io/2a4487d2322bcd7765055a1e9f112eab8dc5beb185e2bb998900e4d8926100a8-91-app-card.png)

  3. Enter a name for your connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/dc696a97ee85b97c370742deac55c66ad73061d5cd1b3df72a0afb69cbec42d2-connection-name.png)

  4. If you haven't connected a LINE Official Account in 91APP, expand the **Connect your LINE Official Account in 91APP Marketing Cloud** section and follow the provided guides to complete the setup.
  5. Click **\+ Choose page(s)** to select the page you want to connect.

![](https://files.readme.io/484b0f23d43c10df7dfc9539d3acd96e4435cb91fae05216dbab07aa7e76ce64-choose-page-line.png)

  6. Input your account details in both your BotBonnie console and your 91APP store settings.



Configuration| Source| Destination| Instruction  
---|---|---|---  
**1\. Input API key and webhook share secret**|  Email from 91APP| BotBonnie console| Enter your **Shop serial number** , **x-api-key** , and **Webhook share secret** into corresponding fields.   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/0b73e9527029dfd70ee6634e0ea213a81363aa7e9f4b0843988cfd6a958dea9d-4-1-credentials.png)  
**2\. Paste API key in your 91APP store’s settings**|  BotBonnie console| 91APP console| 

  1. In BotBonnie, click the copy icon for the **x-api-key** field.
  2. Switch to 91APP's console, navigate to the **Marketing Cloud** > Growth (**流量成長**) > LINE Module (**LINE 模組設定**), paste the copied key into the binding key field (**綁定鑰匙 Key**) and save your changes.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/53abd49dd7365af44a641371e1f75990f01c54359f9b3d11336d1d8382e96238-step2.png)  
**3\. Input 91APP store’s binding link**|  91APP console| BotBonnie console| 

  1. In 91APP, copy the binding link (**綁定連結**) from its console.
  2. Switch to BotBonnie's console and paste the link into the **Binding link** field.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/64cdad1b9bfa60ed727d22a8ccbe4b9756a929d20fe4249e54e6405a74851962-step3.png)  
**4\. Configure webhook URL in your 91APP store settings**|  BotBonnie console| 91APP console| 

  1. In BotBonnie, click the copy icon for the **Webhook URL**.
  2. Switch to 91APP's console and paste the URL into webhook URL (**綁定回傳網址 URL**) field and save your changes.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/4e58b11de1e037c2b6cd6ecba0869ca7996935effe1e50aead153a78425fba4a-step4.png)  
  
  7. Go back to BotBonnie's console, click **Next** in the upper-right corner to continue to set up account linking.



* * *

## 

3\. (Optional) Set up account linking

Configure the following options:

  * Select the **Message of successful linking** checkbox and configure the confirmation message displayed when an account is successfully linked.
  * Select the **Switch menu group or triggered actions after account linking** checkbox to [trigger specific actions](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/triggered-actions) after successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/6ea2eba6f3980a650cbbfb05af3f58794e983733dbdde87a03452606c4437ef6-91-set-up-account-linking.png)

# Cyberbiz

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * LINE
>   * Messenger
> 


Connect your Cyberbiz store to BotBonnie chatbot so users can view their shopping information and receive notifications through supported messaging channels. With this integration, users who link their accounts can:

  * View their [transaction history](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-features-query-transaction-history)
  * Receive [automatic status notifications](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications)



Follow these setup steps to add a connection:

  1. Obtain credentials
  2. Connect with Cyberbiz
  3. Set up account linking
  4. (Optional) Set up notifications



After setup, you can [manage connections](doc:common-settings-integration-e-commerce-integrations#manage-connections) on whenever necessary.

* * *

## 

1\. Obtain credentials

Before you start the integration, you'll need your API credentials (username and secret) from Cyberbiz. Contact the Cyberbiz support team to request these credentials.

* * *

## 

2\. Connect with Cyberbiz

Follow these steps to connect your Cyberbiz store to BotBonnie.

  1. Open the project launcher dropdown in the top navigation bar, select **Common settings** , and then click **EC platforms** in the left navigation panel.

![](https://files.readme.io/42d9e57b124e8eaa22c136bae1bc157fed1c1f4b734c857cb4a8a5c4c63055d4-nav-ec-platforms.png)

  2. Click the **Cyberbiz** card to start the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/a18235677105be340b546fa15b3a1698e325e6321554e64792bf5a0b20cdf230-cyberbiz-add-connection.png)

  3. Enter a name for your connection. Then, click **\+ Choose page(s)** to select the Facebook Messenger or LINE Official Account you want to connect.

![](https://files.readme.io/e9df42d556400f7d2d7877f1fdbe066da77e3bc650536b8754bf1b08d720529f-add-connection-choose-pages.png)

  4. Enter the following Cyberbiz credentials:
     * API username
     * API secret



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Contact the Cyberbiz support team to get these API credentials.

  5. When you're done, select **Next** in the upper-right corner to continue to set up account linking.



* * *

## 

3\. Set up account linking

  1. Under **User login method** , select how users authenticate: **Phone number only** , **Email only** , or **Either phone number or email**.
  2. (Optional) Check the **Apply monthly SMS limit** box and specify the maximum number of SMS verification messages per month to control your budget.
  3. Enter a **Redirect URL** where users can register as members. This URL is essential for initiating the [account linking process](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-integration-account-linking#set-up-an-entry-point-for-account-linking) via chatbot flows or menus.

![](https://files.readme.io/cf065652e2a5c60d05bb9cb32b0655031a6a0bb335a6bcb1f55514e711e649b2-redirect-url.png)

  4. Customize the **Terms and conditions** text to display during the account linking flow.
  5. Configure the following options:
     * **Message of successful linking** : Enable this to send a confirmation message when the system successfully links an account.
     * **Switch menu group or triggered actions after account linking** : Enable to [trigger specific actions](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/triggered-actions) after successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/4dc824c5f30428586a566335a91594211af5103729b22681760ec93f24b854d1-account-linked-actions.png)

  6. When you're done, select **Next** in the upper-right corner to continue to enable automatic status notifications.



* * *

## 

4\. (Optional) Set up notifications

Enable automatic notifications to alert users about order, point and coupon updates to enhance user experience. For detailed configuration options, see [Automatic status notifications](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications).

__Updated 7 months ago

* * *

# Shopify

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * LINE
>   * Messenger
> 


Connect your Shopify store to the BotBonnie chatbot so users can view their shopping information, receive notifications for orders and restocked products. With this integration, users who linked their accounts can view their [transaction history](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-features-query-transaction-history) and receive [automatic status notifications](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications).

Follow these setup steps to add a connection:

  1. Create an API token
  2. Connect with Shopify
  3. Set up account linking
  4. (Optional) Set up notifications



After setup, you can [manage connection](doc:common-settings-integration-e-commerce-integrations#manage-connections) whenever necessary.

* * *

## 

1\. Create an API token

Before connecting BotBonnie to your Shopify store, you'll need to create a custom app in Shopify console and generate an API token. This token allows BotBonnie to securely access your store's data.

  1. Follow [Shopify's guide](https://help.shopify.com/en/manual/apps/app-types/custom-apps) to create a custom app.
  2. When configuring Admin API scopes, enable these required permissions:
     * `read_orders`
     * `write_customers`
     * `read_customers`
     * `read_products`
     * `read_custom_fulfillment_services`
     * `read_fulfillments`
     * `read_merchant_managed_fulfillment_orders`
     * `read_third_party_fulfillment_orders`
     * `read_returns`
     * `read_shipping`



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Copy and save the Admin API access token immediately after installing your as it's only displayed once for security reasons.

* * *

## 

2\. Connect with Shopify

  1. In your BotBonnie console, open the project launcher dropdown in the top navigation bar, select **Common settings** , and then click **EC platforms** in the left navigation panel.

![](https://files.readme.io/42d9e57b124e8eaa22c136bae1bc157fed1c1f4b734c857cb4a8a5c4c63055d4-nav-ec-platforms.png)

  2. Click the **Shopify** card to start the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/6f5a00332395029765e70afd4445d321beb3f6bfeeb61334b01ee4e37bf4f073-add-connection-shopify.png)

  3. Enter connection name and click **\+ Choose page(s)** to select the Messenger or LINE Official Account you want to connect.

![](https://files.readme.io/ee056ef74f4148c653c09b0d94efbd6d9dbbd749641263a7109675e36ee85916-shopify-pages.png)

  4. Enter the following Shopify account details:
     1. Shop ID: Your Shopify store identifier.
     2. API Token: The token you created in step 1.

![](https://files.readme.io/0acf58ddf0055c5d324b8e3f4b8f09b040231ff5fd922c825698bfd751ea872b-shopify-account-details.png)

  5. Click **Next** to continue to set up account linking.



* * *

## 

3\. Set up account linking

  1. Under **User login method** , select how users authenticate: **Phone number only** , **Email only** , or **Either phone number or email**.
  2. (Optional) Check the **Apply monthly SMS limit** and specify the maximum number of SMS verification messages per month to control your budget.
  3. Enter a **Redirect URL** , the webpage where users will register or log into their accounts to complete [account linking](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-integration-account-linking).

![](https://files.readme.io/1d05170a1722f55494e2771791584ef11995308c00f0f5397345abe6c7f4f7a3-shopify-redirect-url.png)

  4. Customize the **Terms and conditions** text to display during the account linking flow.
  5. (Optional) Configure the following options:
     * **Message of successful linking** : Enable to send success message when account linking succeeds.
     * **Switch menu group or triggered actions after account linking** : Activate specific [trigger action](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/triggered-actions) after successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/4dc824c5f30428586a566335a91594211af5103729b22681760ec93f24b854d1-account-linked-actions.png)

  6. Click **Next** in to continue to set up notifications.



* * *

## 

4\. (Optional) Set up notifications

Enable automatic notifications to alert users about order and stock updates to enhance user experience. For detailed configuration options, see [Automatic status notifications](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications).

__Updated 7 months ago
